#### 10.15.5 Binding implementation contract — physical lifecycle, semantic checkpoint and viewer geometry are separate clocks

**Single-owner rule:** For one validated (roomCode, interactionId, rootFrameId, rootEventId, physicalCardId, actualAuthorId), only one **physical root presentation coordinator** may render the public root. It must persist across NEGATION, GROUP_RESOLUTION participant 1/2/3, damage, optional triggered skill and settlement, even when presentationRevision, checkpointId, participant, response timer, selection branch or notice changes. Build this linkage from PROVEN server/public relationships; never join by latest timeline item, card kind, player name or turnSeat. If evidence of the linkage is missing, fail closed rather than guess. Other unrelated card families may still use their generic fallback; a root already owned/parked by a Journey must **never** return through static fallback.

| Independent clock | Stable authority | Events allowed to change it | Forbidden side effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Physical event identity** | Proven root event + physical card + true author + typed interaction/frame proof | A different genuinely committed physical card | Duplicate or impersonated root |
| **Visual lifecycle** | Same physical event state: UNSEEN → REVEAL → MOVE → PARKED → RETIRED | First public commitment, normal ≈4s reveal, ≈380ms move, real interrupt or retirement | PARKED→REVEAL from a new checkpoint, response, poll or switching viewers |
| **Semantic checkpoint** | PROVEN interaction/decision/current participant/settlement | Server advances group target, Negation, damage, outcome | Restart visual root or move already parked root |
| **Viewer layout epoch** | Viewer ID + measured Seat/Dock/topology + visualViewport | TEST SWITCH, reconnect, resize/rotation, topology changes | Old-view-to-new-view flying animation; wrong author |
| **Public narration** | Latest proven public stage/event copy via H4 | Genuine server-proven semantic stage change | Text follows shrinking root, stale previous-stage notice, false private reveal |

**Q2 / state ownership:** extend one typed Group-root Journey throughout normal Group response and GROUP_RESOLVED, including phases where Negation is not active. Preserve existing exact source/root/card/frame proof, root actor identity, historical genuine Negations and distinct physical response cards. A genuinely NEW committed response card may reveal at centre and interrupt the root; the old root remains PARKED. The old root must not be reselected as a new central event by an unrelated target response or settlement result. Implement arbitration once at a shared presenter boundary, not separately per group stage. Do not merely hide CSS static cards while still mounting duplicate physical owners.

**Q3 / first-paint view barrier:** visual phase/commit history belongs to the root, while layout coordinates belong to the CURRENT viewer. On TEST SWITCH or legitimate layout-epoch change, reproject already PARKED roots to the new measured true-author anchor BEFORE first paint, with transition disabled. Do not animate from cached actualCardRect belonging to another viewer, do not flash at stale coordinates and do not replay 4s because an event is new to the observer. If new anchor is unmeasurable, postpone that card's painting rather than use 0,0 or guess. A newly confirmed physical card in the same, unchanged view still gets the accepted 4s/380ms motion. Test on Attack/Dodge, Duel/Lust and other Journey types too, while keeping Q3 a separate bounded task.

**Q4 / H4 independent public caption:** use one stage-fixed, safely located PublicCardAnnouncement band, sourced only from up-to-date proven public checkpoints. It may change copy as response windows genuinely advance; it must not change its position because a card shrinks, parks or a timer ticks. Retire old-stage copy as soon as authoritative semantic scope changes (e.g. no leftover "Negation can be played" during Group response); do not reveal private submissions. Measure the actual newly deployed H4 implementation, accept existing H3 font/copy, and preserve USER-ACCEPTED U13-B activity chip. H4 centre deviation ≤12px and vertical movement ≤16px apply under unchanged safe geometry; avoid top Seats, current central/parked physical cards, timer, Local Dock and chip.

**Q1 instrumentation additions:** privacy-reviewed diagnostic trace should log paired PROVEN server and presenter IDs and this public-only record per render checkpoint: build SHA, session ID, monotonic elapsed time, room/interaction/frame/checkpoint/revision, root event/card/author, current participant, current viewer/layout epoch, rendererKind/motionType/visualPhase, reveal/move start and elapsed, number + DOM path/rect of every SAME-root full/parked instance, last viewer rect versus current anchor rect, H4 notice event/semantic stage/text key + rect, safe stage/obstacle rects. Redact all private Hand identities, other players' Negation availability/intents, credentials, secret roles. Instrument ahead of behavior changes and avoid treating presentationRevision as a new physical-card identity.

**Deterministic acceptance invariants across each sampled paint:**

1. Exactly one real, newly committed root reveals centrally, moves to proven author once, then remains PARKED during all later target responses; zero old-root central full-size instances after PARKED. New separately committed physical response is allowed centrally.
2. Root presenter retains constant physical ID/author under all server checkpoints and up to final proven retirement; renderer arbitration prevents both a live physical Journey and an independent same-card static fallback.
3. Post-switch PARKED card's **first painted** x/y is its newly measured actual-author anchor (≤2px rounding tolerance), not any interpolated point from previous-view x/y; no re-reveal/clock reset.
4. Exactly one current public narration for a given stage, with current proven copy, H4-safe geometry and no collision with accepted U13-B chip, HP, countdown, Seat histories or Local Hand.
5. Server rules, CurrentAction legal controls, full physical conservation, Policy-B timing, multi-seat Negation Plan A privacy, R1/R2/Da Qiao/Lust and existing accepted UX remain unchanged.

**Mandatory proof:** run a real authenticated 4-client Worker/D1 Barbarian Invasion and Raining Arrows through Negation, 3 sequential participant responses, damage/trigger and final settlement; match the EXACT root/card/author IDs across clients (arrival times need not match exactly). Separately stress TEST SWITCH 1→2→3→4→1, both during initial reveal and after parking; reconnect/late join, slow polls, viewport 390×700, 390×844, captured 440×766, 440×894 and 480×900, both 4-seat top row and 6/8/10 side-seat topology, reduced motion. Capture DOM states/frame rects/caption history rather than rely on smoke or still screenshots alone. Do not confuse a single TEST SWITCH browser with four real clients. Exact-SHA CI/Worker version and tests must be actually observed; if Q1's current explicit ban on local automated tests remains, honour it and label unverified claims. Technical delivery is PENDING HUMAN REVIEW.

**Sequence and task ownership:** Current HANDOVER.md Q1 is still IN PROGRESS, with diagnostic changes reportedly local-only. **Do not reset, overwrite or begin Q2 in place of that uncommitted work.** Q1 → Q2 → Q3 → Q4 are four individually bounded Coding Agent tasks; finish one and then replace the handoff with the next. The first thing Agent must do on resumption is re-read this latest design revision and preserve its local Q1 instrumentation. No implementation, post-fix game verification, new H4 failure or user visual acceptance is claimed by this document revision.


#### 10.15.6 Executable three-surface presentation specification — MUST / MUST NOT (2026-10-17 clarification)

**P0 user requirement in one sentence:** For the **same proven real card**, the (A) central full-sized card, (B) returned small author-attached card and (C) shared public explanatory text must describe one coherent public event across time and viewpoints; they must not independently restart, move between owners, disagree, or resurrect old stages. This section is **normative over cosmetic placement preferences** and can be handed directly to the Coding Agent. The problem is **presentation arbitration and rehydration, not changes to the WTK rules or CurrentAction**.

**Three surfaces have distinct jobs and must NEVER compete for authority:**

| Surface | When it exists | Where it belongs | Exactly what it may display | What it must NEVER do |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **A. Centre REVEAL / MOVE CardFace** | Only for a **newly committed, server-proven physical card** whose event has not already completed its current viewer's reveal/move lifecycle; newer committed cards may interrupt | Central battlefield safe reveal area, then animate toward true author | ONE current full-size real card with real card ID, owner and event ID | Rerender an old parked root because the group advances to the next target; invent a second root from settlement; run again after TEST SWITCH/poll/refresh |
| **B. PARKED miniature/history** | The same physical event after MOVE, or immediately on viewing already-committed history after switching/late joining/reconnecting | Actual **author's** Seat or own Dock in this observer's current topology (global chronological ordering retained) | The same real physical card, shrunk once, with genuine ordinal when applicable | Fly from a previous observer's seat; change owner; grow back into a centre card; disappear merely because next participant must answer |
| **C. Public event narration (H4)** | While there is a **current, proven public stage/event** requiring a shared explanation; update or retire as proven semantic stage changes | Its **own fixed central safe reading band** (not a child of the card's position/transform) | Current proven actor, action, target, response or outcome text; accepted H3 typography/copy | Follow A or B; lag with a prior Negation/target message; duplicate a separate generic toast; overlap A/B, player HP, response countdown, Dock or accepted U13-B status plaque |

**Strict state-transition contract for one root:**

~~~text
PROVEN NEW ROOT [event/card/author] -> REVEAL(max 4000ms by Policy B)
                                  -> MOVE(about 380ms, to THIS viewer's true-author anchor)
                                  -> PARKED (same real card; stay parked through all target decisions)
                                  -> RETIRED (only when proven history-display policy retires it)

PARKED + target-2/3 response = PARKED (no large re-display)
PARKED + GROUP_RESOLVED      = PARKED (no new static large card)
PARKED + presentationRevision/checkpoint changes = PARKED
PARKED + TEST SWITCH / resize = PARKED at recalculated destination, SNAP WITHOUT TRANSITION
PARKED + poll / reconnect / remount = PARKED, never replay by default
NEW different committed response card = its OWN new centre lifecycle; prior root stays PARKED

C: Proven public semantic stage N -> stage N+1 = replace/retire stage-N text;
   C position remains in its safe band and is not coupled to A/B transforms.
~~~

**Timing and interruption:** The intended REVEAL hold is **up to approximately 4 seconds**, and MOVE about **380ms**, not an unconditional 4-second blocking delay. A newer, *genuinely committed and publicly proven* response may interrupt the prior centre reveal; no purely local response intent or transient UI checkpoint may do so. No new card? Then no fresh REVEAL. A network poll, target index advance, damage/skill settlement, stage revision or visual viewport change is **not** a new physical commitment. Reduced-motion users see equivalent identity/placement without forced flight. Do not infer timing/ownership from a DOM timer.

**Cross-player contract using the recorded genuine Barbarian root:**

| State / viewer | Expected A centre | Expected B miniature | Expected C narration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Viewer 0 first sees root 76af7aee… newly committed | One initial REVEAL, then MOVE | Same root parks under actual source 72383b7b… | Initial **server-proven** public root/action message; H4 safe band |
| Viewer 0 watches target 2, then target 3 respond | **No second root** at centre (new genuine response CardFaces only if proved) | Root d7478792… remains under that same author | Text advances to the newly proven response/target; previous text removed |
| TEST SWITCH viewer 0→1→2→3 during group response | **No root REVEAL restart** | Same physical root **snaps directly** to each new viewer's measured author Seat/Dock, zero flight from prior layout | Current public semantic message reprojects into the viewer's **own safe H4 band**, does not trail the card |
| Group settles | **No 88×128px old-root static fallback** | Root history remains on true author until normal proven retirement | Only current group settlement outcome; obsolete response/Negation message gone |
| Newly connected independent client after root was already public | **No historical replay as fresh commitment** | Reconstructed parked root in the actual author slot according to the existing history policy | Latest proven public stage, not delayed or cached private copy |

**Renderer exclusivity is a functional requirement, not a styling tweak:** At any observation of a given physical root, the system must have a **single effective visual owner** (one typed Journey rather than Journey competing with static-fallback). No full-size static root CardFace is permitted once that root is PARKED, even if a Group settlement produces a different action object; no CSS-only hiding of a duplicate which is still independently driving stale notice state. Display identity must use proven interaction/root/card/author linkage and **must not depend on** checkpointId, presentationRevision, current target seat, current player name, current viewerId or DOM coordinates. Those fields can update **semantics** or **layout**, not reset the root's visual clock.

**Presentation and geometry must be separated:** The root's logical phase/event history survives updates. On a viewerId/Seat topology/visualViewport change, invalidate old coordinates, measure new DOM anchors, and suppress transitions **before painting** any already parked historical card. A client may have one viewer-relative position per layout epoch, but may NEVER interpolate from previous-view x=338px to new-view x=196px for an already PARKED card. If current true-author anchor is unavailable, wait/withhold rendering; never guess location/owner. Same rule applies to legacy Attack/Dodge, Negation, Duel/Lust and other real physical card Journeys, without expanding Q2 beyond Group roots.

**Public narration freshness:** The H4 text's semantic event ID/checkpoint must track current **PROVEN** server interaction state. At confirmed advance, replace the prior stage's visible copy atomically: e.g. “Waiting for Negation” cannot linger when response ownership has advanced to a proven group target; “target 1 responded” cannot still be the visible current instruction while target 2 is acting. The narration's placement is determined by a safe stage band; it must not be recalculated from A's shrinking card or B's parked anchor. When geometry changes (e.g. viewer switch) the safe band may legitimately reproject, but under **unchanged layout** its centre drifts no more than 12px horizontally and 16px vertically. Preserve the separate USER-ACCEPTED U13-B bottom activity chip; H4 provides *event meaning*, U13-B provides *whose action is awaited*.

**Source evidence vs current release:** The uploaded old-build aa71e75 trace proves root REVEAL t=72.729, MOVE t=76.791, PARKED t=77.162, then later same-root full card sightings at t≈87.79 and 116.63; TEST SWITCH records x=338.3→196→53.7→14 for the same parked root. This evidence **does not by itself certify present H4 malfunction** or real four-client simultaneity. Audit the **deployed new SHA**. From latest source, untyped normal Group/settlement candidates and PublicCardDisplay static-fallback remain the main Q1/Q2 suspects; do not claim a bug fixed by specification alone.

**Concrete Coding Agent acceptance gate, not optional:**

1. **Q1 (current only):** Diagnose and log ONE root's stable `rootEventId`, `physicalCardId`, `actualAuthorId`, `interactionId`, `rootFrameId` from PROVEN server projection to chosen renderer, with `rendererKind`, `motionType`, `visualPhase`, `viewer/layoutEpoch` and all same-physical-card DOM paths/rects. Record whether a static fallback actually reappears after PARKED on **latest Worker**. Keep current uncommitted diagnostics safe; no behavior edit in Q1.
2. **Q2:** Typed one-owner GROUP-root lifecycle through normal group target resolution and final settlement; guarantee zero old-root static resurrection and correct 4s/380ms behaviour. Dedicated browser regression must fail if any frame contains an old full-size root after PARKED.
3. **Q3:** All historical PARKED cards rebase in the **first newly painted frame** after TEST SWITCH (or layout epoch change), anchor error ≤2px, no intermediate old-view flight, no lifecycle reset.
4. **Q4:** Verify *latest* H4 (minimal fix **only if reproduced**) in 4 independent authenticated Worker/D1 clients plus a separate TEST SWITCH browser. At each target step confirm narration semantics, only one current H4 banner, fixed safe position, no collision with card/history, top Seats, timers, hand and U13-B chip.
5. Capture matched **real event/card/author IDs** across clients for Barbarian Invasion **and** Raining Arrows through ≥3 actual target responses, Negation, damage and final settlement; separately test viewer switch both during initial reveal and after park, late join/refresh/resize, long names and small phones (390×700, 390×844, captured 440×766, 440×894, 480×900). Distinguish screenshots, instrumented DOM/trace, exact-SHA GitHub CI and **actual deployment**. A green build alone does not satisfy this gate.
6. **Stop conditions:** if the public linkage/author is unproven, fail closed and report—not guessed render; if Q1 shows the current release is already correct, write evidence and NO CHANGE rather than rewrite H4; if the intended fix changes CurrentAction, deck rules, hidden Negation information, existing accepted UX or server protocol, stop for review. Mark each code delivery **PENDING HUMAN REVIEW**, never “accepted” without fresh user-phone sign-off.

**Boundaries:** This specification authorizes **presentation** fixes only. Existing game's verified decisions, exact physical-card conservation, shared Negation Plan A, accepted H3 and U13-B, reveal interruption and real chronological author parking remain intact. Q1 is the sole active HANDOVER task; Q2–Q4 are future sequential tasks, **not** permission to start all four together.

**Human retest after Q1–Q4:** Observe ONE Barbarian Invasion over ALL target responses and switch TEST Player 1→2→3→4; a real root returns under REAL author exactly once, never re-enlarges/re-animates on SWITCH or target advance, all viewers see same public timeline, and narration remains in a stage-fixed safe band while U13-B accurately shows who is awaited.

### 10.16 EM-01 — safe spectator interaction (USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED, 2026-10-22)

**Accepted UX:** During Harvest/other shared choice panels, only the true server-authorized chooser may select or Confirm. Other authenticated viewers retain TEST SWITCH and harmless public Hero/Card inspection; blank overlay areas **do not trap taps**. Spectators never gain an actor's private candidates or gameplay authority. Preserve H4, real card history, server validation and privacy. Earlier diagnostics and repair steps are historical only.

### 10.17 — Battlefield Choice/HUD family (BCP-1, BCP-2, INS-5 and BH5 accepted; BCP-3 / HUD-4 delivered, pending phone review)

**Formal choices:** Product owner approves **A**, a single compact **Battlefield Choice Panel** within the play-table combat area (not a whole-screen takeover); Confirm/Use and legally required negative action belong INSIDE its footer. Product owner approves **A1** for Bumper Harvest: a compact spectator READ-ONLY view still shows the *actually public* revealed card pool and the proven taken status, while only the authorized chooser can select or Confirm. For every picker, **re-tap a selected card to deselect**. A separate **Cancel** is exceptionally rare and NOT part of default UX; do not automatically replace it with a new Clear button. Optional server-authorized Skip means formally declining a choice, and never appears alongside a redundant Cancel. The older §5.5 full-screen/modal/Cancel wording is superseded for visual presentation by this section; its authoritative selection rules still apply.

**Five screenshot review reports, NOT code acceptance:** IMG_3288(1).jpeg: Bumper Harvest large central card/card presentation obscures revealed-choice row, no clearly readable action/choice arrangement; IMG_3290.jpeg: top opponent Equipment positions shift with occupancy, small sword nearly horizontal; IMG_3292.jpeg: Sima Yi Inspector portrait too small, right details and Judgement/Equipment misallocated; IMG_3294.jpeg: Burning Bridges picker consumes too much height, controls below visible area, face-up Equipment lacks independent Info; IMG_3295.jpeg: Retaliation huge panel with Use/Skip/Cancel redundancies. The files are human UX observations; do not infer their current live build SHA or assume the source of overlapping card layers without fresh reproduction.

**EM-01 prior delivery / ownership boundary (HISTORICAL; EM-01 USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED 2026-10-22):** EM-01 Harvest pointer pass-through was technically deployed at `32b22b829c1bfd59a4b533fa36a86b0334a3ad64`, and shared overlays at `7cc761c34ecdbbe12e7bb4af852a3975d8873206` with reported CI runs `39078356730` / `39081904968`. These are not user-approved. Preserve their TEST SWITCH, actor permission, privacy, nonmodal focus and hit-test protections. Current Agent handover still has EM-01 review open; start new UX slices at a clean boundary after reconciling latest remote source, with no worktree overwrite. Design feedback is not permission for Planner to modify HANDOVER.md or code.

#### 10.17.0 BCP-0 — common selection UX contract (ALL scoped extra-choice dialogs)

- **Physical layout:** A compact bordered dark/gold floating component positioned INSIDE the measured battle/interaction-safe region, below actual opponent seats, above the Local Dock/Hand/Guidance and outside the timer and accepted H4 public narration. No viewport-sized opaque sheet, no global touch overlay, no click intercept outside bounded controls. At 390×700 and 440×766 the footer must be visible at first view without page scrolling; large content is scrollable INSIDE the card area, preferably horizontal rows. Header one short skill/effect title + one instruction, minimal extra filler. Use consistent card aspect ratios and scales (may be smaller than ordinary hand cards, still legible through Details), close spacing and responsive safe geometry.
- **Selection:** Use server-given eligible card/opaque position keys, min/max, target, and current decision actor. Gold outline + check + modest raised position for selected. First tap adds, **repeat tap same key removes**; a different tap changes one-card choice, multi-choice selection toggles individually. Limit selection according to authoritative min/max. Selected count concise (e.g. `1 / 2`). **Confirm disabled until exactly server-permitted amount**, submits once under action revision guard. Tapping Info does NOT toggle/select/submit. No client-generated extra hidden Hand keys.
- **Buttons:** Default mandatory decision footer = **Confirm ONLY**, plus tap-to-deselect. Optional server-permitted reaction footer = **Use/Confirm + Skip ONLY**, Skip calls the exact authoritative decline action; do not show Cancel. **Do not add Clear/Reset by default.** An independently real, legitimate *abort of the whole uncommitted flow* may exceptionally use Cancel only after the Agent identifies the specific legal semantics and seeks product owner approval; do not equate Cancel with deselect or Skip. Existing internal onCancel function may remain as draft cleanup for viewer/session change, but should not be rendered as a redundant visible button. A genuinely complex drag/reorder-only Reset requires separate proof, not automatic generalization.
- **Safe viewing/privacy:** Spectators may SWITCH/Inspect real public Heroes, their own Hand Details and public Equipment where allowed, but must not gain an unauthorized selection or see hidden identities. Hand backs remain anonymous with existing `hand:n` or single random-hand server key. Information icons appear only on genuinely public identifiable choice cards and are wholly inside the card edge, accessible by touch and keyboard. Info view can close without dismissing pending choice. Respect current session/actor when switching TEST viewers: do not leak or submit stale drafts; preserve server deadlines/confirmed picks.
- **Different content, consistent frame:** Standard 1-card target picker (Steal/Burning Bridges), multi-card skill picker (Frost Sword), revealed public pool (Harvest), assignment (Legacy), rearrangement (Stargazing) may use different inner controls but the SAME title/card/footer vocabulary and bounded nonmodal geometry. Keep existing straightforward local Equipment-cost selection in Local Dock instead of forcing unnecessary duplicate popup. Actual per-effect rules and W5/W6 public effect Frame review remain distinct.

**Reproduce** with latest live Worker/D1 4-player authentic Harvest, at least two choices, one current chooser and one spectator. Capture exact DOM/paint geometry of central genuine root CardFace, public H4 narration, Harvest pool, card buttons and footer across reveal→choice→Confirm→next actor. Determine first *actual* overlap, rather than assume one z-index rule.

**Required:** Keep the true large Harvest root reveal/move valid but never paint it on TOP of active selectable public cards; stage the root and pool without overlap/duplicate fake cards. Compact the choice panel within battlefield safe space. Real revealed options fully visible/readable, horizontal swipe when necessary. Each public card has a small inset **Info** affordance that opens its genuine card description without selecting/confirming. Real selection lifts/highlights; same card re-tap deselects. A clearly visible **Confirm choice** in actor footer must stay accessible at 390×700 without any page scroll; no Cancel/Clear (choice mandatory). Taken cards become disabled/dimmed with truthful public taken-by status.
**A1 spectators:** Keep compact **read-only** public pool + taken status + safe public waiting label; no actor Confirm/Skip or private preview; allow harmless public-card Info and all SWITCH/Inspector clicks. No hidden information leakage, timers/confirmed choices unchanged.
**Acceptance:** Actual 390×700/390×844/440×766/480×900 phone-like screenshots, DOM overlap check during 2+ picks, real pointer tap on SWITCH while nonactor, Info does not change selected card, choice→re-tap→choice→one Confirm legal, no root or H4 regression.

#### 10.17.5 INS-5 — HISTORICAL parent design; refined and user-accepted under §10.17.5.1 INS-5-F1-R (NO NEW TASK)

**Observed:** IMG_3292 Sima Yi Inspector has a narrow portrait and judgement/equipment sections sharing the lower space inefficiently. Existing Inspector has real 2-slot Judgement and 4-slot Equipment, public skills and working accepted detail navigation; keep those semantics.

**Required:** Inspector left column approximately **25–33% width** for one large true Hero illustration (correct aspect ratio, no distortion), visually prominent. Right **67–75%** area has **two stacked levels**: upper = same existing hero name and HP/Hand count plus skills **vertical compact narrow buttons**; use remaining top-right width for exactly **two small Judgement card positions** (overflow for any third+ real Judgement, never silently hide). Lower-right = Equipment row in fixed Weapon, Armour, +1/−1 Horse order, with authentic illustrated public cards and clickable existing Details. No extra full-screen layer or loss of accepted §8.5 skill/card detail toggles, privacy or Inspector x close.
**Tests:** zero/multiple skills, 0/2/3+ Judgement, all four Equipment slots, long names, 390×700/440×894, 4/6/8/10-seat, Card Details/skill descriptions, safe Dock and SWITCH, portrait truly larger on phone.
**One task:** `UX2-10.17-INS5-INSPECTOR-TWO-LEVEL-01`.

Keep the compact **two Judgement above four Equipment** arrangement on the right rather than a tall or one-row full-width band. Switching Hero or public card details updates the existing panel without nested popups or false identities. Maintain private/public boundaries and legal board touch. Only `CurrentAction` determines who can Confirm/Skip. No public duplication, hidden information leak or client-owned game rules. **Identical accepted feature to §10.17.2**, not a new task.

## 11. UX3 FUTURE backlog — Negation Plan B, immediate-public-on-Confirm (NOT AN UX2 TASK)

**User preference recorded 2026-10-14 (future UX3 only):** Explore individual immediate-public Negation Confirm through a separately approved future UX3 design. This is NOT authorized autonomous UX2 work. UX2 Negation Plan A remains private all-seat submission, maximum 8s and early server settlement only after EVERY living seat explicitly responds.

**Non-trivial unresolved tradeoff:** UX2 private simultaneous intents still use canonical seat priority; all-seats explicit early completion doesn't change that. Immediately displaying an INDIVIDUAL's Confirm as a PUBLIC card, while others remain undecided, could expose a losing intent, consume a wrong card or leak secret availability. UX3 requires a separately approved authoritative arbitration rule, not an optimistic animation or simply changing the 8s maximum.

**UX3 questions to design with user before authorization:** (1) first-arrival commitment versus stable clockwise seat priority / override grace window; (2) protecting opponent Negation availability and private Hand when no card is submitted; (3) new counter windows, timeouts, fairness under network lag and reconnect; (4) atomic exactly-once actual card consumption, conflicting simultaneous confirms, Duel/Group/Judgement consistency; (5) visible chronological author-attached card history, animation interruption, old-save migration, rollbacks and authenticated multi-viewer privacy tests.

**Separate UX3 design decision, not an UX2 review gate:** UX3 needs distinct product/rules authorization because it changes priority, privacy and fairness. Continue all approved UX2 work without awaiting acceptance of prior UX2 items, but do not silently implement this unapproved UX3 rule.


### 10.24 ST-RING-DIR-1 — TOP-FIRST clockwise phone ring (USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED, 2026-10-28 08:21 UTC; 5/5 checks)

**Accepted UX:** True viewer-relative clockwise ring follows server Seat identity, the four-player reference order, TEST SWITCH and correct target/Inspector/author mapping. Phone opponent allocation is **TOP-FIRST**: **5 players = 4 top, 6 = 5 top, 7 = exactly 4 top + 1 left + 1 right; 8 = top-first, remainder on sides**. Safe spacing must protect public cards, picker, Hand, H4 and legal taps. Nine/ten-player mapping has technical tests, not independent owner-phone sign-off; 4/6/8 remains the requested visual review scope, without removing game-supported counts. **H2-G3's newer eight-player TOP5 cap and SIDE frame-width/art parity are separately scoped and not accepted by this original ring verdict.**

### 10.25 NEG-COPY-1 — Shared Negation public UX (Duel-path R6 USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED; other stratagems NOT YET TESTED)

**Identity / scope:** `UX2-10.25-NEG-COPY-1-UNIFIED-NEGATION-EFFECT-COPY`. Owner's real-iPhone acceptance on **2026-10-31 09:55 UTC** closes the delivered **Duel/Negation R6 flow and R6-ARCH-01 PAC/H4 correction**; the separately approved **R6-ARCH-02 private Hand receipt** was accepted at **09:01 UTC**. The previously accepted baseline **NEG-A1 (§10.19)**, H4, BCP-1, R2 and W3-F2-R1 stays closed. **The larger original §10.25 also covers Bumper Harvest and other stratagem-card Negation. Those card-specific flows were not individually accepted by this Duel test and remain PENDING OWNER TEST, not a new coding task.** The two R6 implementation corrections are finished; do not reopen them merely because a different card has not yet been reviewed.

**History:** All earlier F1-R1/R2/R3, F1-R4/R5, R6A–D, QA-01/02/03, ARCH-01/02 diagnosis, Agent steps, failed screenshots, tests and deployment journals remain in the immutable [pre-closure §10.25 design](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/e493e97ff1a032262ba74d8736ee16279c91c6aa/docs/UX2-refine.md). These are **history**, not the active source of product rules or a queue of tasks. Current rules below supersede the earlier two-line **H4 event + potential counter** copy: the potential next action belongs to **PAC only**.

#### 10.25.1 — Authoritative event architecture and visual lifecycle (approved baseline)

- **Server facts are the only public truth.** Use proof-graded `PresentationSnapshot` public event facts and linked root/interaction/frame/checkpoint/revision/event IDs, causal actor/target, effect direction and the actual card identity where already public. `CurrentAction`, a private lock, local timer at zero, DOM card position, response count parity without committed evidence, an old timeline string and the current viewer's Hand cannot manufacture a public outcome. Fail closed on missing/contradictory proof. Same public checkpoint must have **the same semantic H4/PAC across TEST SWITCH viewers**, regardless of viewer-relative Seat position.
- **Single responsibility: H4 = what HAS publicly happened.** One latest committed public play, Negation cancel/restore, damage/prevention, publicly proven Hero/equipment outcome or other true effect, with authentic actor and affected recipient. H4 **does not** append future guidance such as `Negation can be played`, `a Negation response is open`, `Another Negation can restore...`, `Waiting for...`, `currently`, `again` or `now`. In full-size, compact and VoiceOver/accessible copy, avoid internal status/protocol jargon. An unavailable newer event must not cause a fabricated success, a duplicate banner, or a rollback to a superseded root.
- **PAC = what happens NEXT, publicly.** One viewer-independent lower battlefield status and next legal public response/participant based on server proof, such as `Waiting for counter responses` and `Negation can cancel/restore the Duel effect on [target].` Neither PAC nor the timer is proof that an effect already occurred. **One player's private locked Pass/Negation must never change the public PAC** or imply that their card was played before public commitment.
- **Console = authenticated viewer's own decision.** The local Dock alone shows actual legal Negation/Pass, Duel Attack/Take Damage, authorized Hero/Skip and selection controls; a server-confirmed current-window private lock may say `You chose to play Negation. Waiting for the other players.` or `You passed. Waiting for the other players.` No nonparticipant or other TEST SWITCH seat sees another player's private intent, hidden Hand, or fake public result. For Treachery keep concise instruction `Use Treachery to obtain the card that caused the damage, or Skip.`; avoid `YOUR DECISION` and redundant `No action needed` banners where no genuine choice exists.
- **Public physical card journey is unique and server-authored.** A genuine public played CardFace reveals once in the centre (Policy-B normally **up to ~4s**, interruptible by a newer committed response), then the **same** physical card moves (normal move around **380ms**) and PARKS as one author-attached history at its actual Seat/Dock. Duel, successive genuine Negations and subsequent Duel Attacks preserve public ordinals and ownership, with top Seat stacks descending, side Seats fanning inward, local Dock history above Hand. Never render a second legacy centre thumbnail for the same physical event, put a card under the wrong author, replay the root after a real response, or keep an old root/Attack gallery in the centre during damage or Treachery/Skip.
- **Event/lifecycle ownership is distinct from placement.** Current server-proven interaction stage and latest event dominate: root reveal → real Negation responses → proven settlement/handoff → Duel exchange → proven damage/complete. Root/Negation H4 retires when a real later stage takes ownership; another real committed event may interrupt and supersede a still-pending H4, without chronological replay of obsolete events. **No forced H4 in a stage with no new public result**: PAC alone may express that the duel exchange is awaiting a participant. Placement failure, temporary resize, timer mounting or private viewer choice cannot make an unsuperseded committed H4 disappear permanently or mark it as shown.
- **Other stratagems keep the same proof boundaries**, but must respect their own true effect and recipient (e.g., Bumper Harvest Negation blocks/restores the **current authorized recipient's one pick**, not the entire shared Harvest pool or all recipients). A real final settlement closes the counter window; no H4/PAC claims an extra response, damage, completed pick or legal choice without server proof. During active Harvest Negation, the pool remains read-only; only the real authorized recipient may choose after response settlement. This generic contract does not assert those other-card phone flows have passed review.

#### 10.25.2 — Approved copy, layout and privacy decisions

**Duel / counter-Negation — example public English copy** (substitute each **server-proven** player, real target and effect; the H4 examples are event-only and the PAC examples are next-step-only):

| Actual public stage/fact | H4 — committed event only | PAC — next public step |
| --- | --- | --- |
| King genuinely played Duel on Test Player 4 | `King played Duel on Test Player 4.` | `Waiting for counter responses` / `Negation can cancel the Duel effect on Test Player 4.` when the response window really is open |
| First **committed** Negation cancelled the Duel | `Test Player 3's Negation cancels King's Duel effect on Test Player 4.` | `Waiting for counter responses` / `Another Negation can restore the Duel effect on Test Player 4.` while countering is still allowed |
| Second **committed** counter-Negation restored the Duel | `Test Player 2 counters Test Player 3's Negation. The Duel effect on Test Player 4 is restored.` | `Waiting for counter responses` / `Another Negation can cancel the Duel effect on Test Player 4.` while still open |
| Later committed counter | The genuine actor/previous counter and correct newly **cancelled/restored** effect, without next-action suffix | Truthful potential next counter only while the server window is still open; no possible counter after FINAL |
| Proven `DUEL_EXCHANGE` | Retire stale Duel/Negation H4; H4 may describe **a newly committed Duel Attack**, not replay root or a persistent `The Duel takes effect.` | `Waiting for [proved responder] to play Attack or take damage` / `Duel Exchange` |
| Proven damage/prevention or Hero result | True actor, recipient, damage/prevention or generic committed skill outcome; no fabricated success for Skip | Next server-confirmed public stage/actor, never private skill eligibility |

**Bumper Harvest and other stratagems — shared *design* contract, further iPhone validation pending:** The authentic root public H4 states `[actor] played Bumper Harvest.`. Once the pool and real current recipient are established, PAC may announce `Waiting for [recipient] to pick a card` or `Negation can prevent [recipient] from picking.` **only when that is the proven public next step**. A committed first Negation produces H4 `[actor]'s Negation blocks [recipient]'s pick.`; PAC may say `Another Negation can restore [recipient]'s pick.`. A committed counter produces H4 `[actor] counters [prior actor]'s Negation. [recipient]'s pick is restored.`, with PAC indicating a further block only if another response is legal. The same structure extends to later linked counters. After actual final closure, PAC stops offering Negation; do not claim a card was picked merely because a Negation window closed. These are **normative product expectations, NOT a USER-ACCEPTED Harvest test result**.

**Public caption geometry and priority:**
- **H4** has one fixed battlefield-relative horizontal centre/width for unchanged viewport and 4/6/8-seat topology; it may **grow or shrink vertically** with natural readable wrapping, including 3+ lines. Its horizontal position must not move because of text length, countdown, public CardFace movement, PAC or benign polling. For a safe 440 CSS-px four-player stage, use the visibly adequate 320–340px middle lane (normally 340px), not an unexplained 258px narrow box. **H4 may partially overlay an active genuine large public CardFace**, must describe that same public event, and paints **over** authentic PARKED miniatures; use a moderately translucent readable background, preserve recognizable card artwork, avoid consuming card/control touches, and protect Inspector, timer, private picker and actionable UI.
- **PAC** remains centred low near the Stage/Console boundary, readable and stable in **width, centre and intended vertical lane** across countdown appearance/disappearance and short/long message. At a safely unobstructed 440px four-player viewport aim for **340 CSS px**; 320–340px only if real Seat/other protected bounds justify it. The countdown **yields to PAC**, not vice versa: a compact readable clock near the **far-right** (roughly 36–42px where actual geometry allows), or a safe **right-edge vertical relocation** for narrower screens and 6/8-seat side columns. The clock must not shrink PAC, overlap the caption, cover Inspector or legal controls, disappear, or alter the real authorized countdown. **PAC may overlay PARKED miniatures but never an actively enlarged public REVEAL**. H4 and PAC must not cover one another or obscure a genuine timer/control.
- When real protected geometry prevents readable placement, keep the latest current public message pending with a bounded recovery path and no false acceptance, rather than inventing smaller illegible fonts, trimming event names or shifting H4 left/right. Preserve reduced-motion/VoiceOver semantics and avoid a second same-event upper-right Hero toast or old `PRIVATE DRAW` title. Geometry on 6/8-player phones must be safe with actual side Seats; do not treat unmeasured side-topology widths as a universal 340px promise.

**Obtained-card privacy — R6-ARCH-02 USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED:** Every genuine committed new card-to-Hand transfer (ordinary/skill draw, gain, steal, recovery or multi-card award) preserves original physical card identity, provenance, actual recipient and server card conservation. The authorized recipient sees the true CardFace **once** privately, briefly, before it settles into Hand; no visible `PRIVATE DRAW` heading, no replay on repeated polling/refresh or TEST SWITCH of old transfer events. Only the recipient's authorized private projection carries concealed card faces/IDs. Public H4 may announce **generic proven gain/skill result** (e.g., `Test Player 4 used Treachery and obtained the card that caused the damage.`), **never `obtained Duel` or the concealed gained card's specific identity**. Separate already-public played/equipped card journeys remain public according to their original proof and true author, not reinterpreted as a new play by the acquiring player. This closed contract must not be reopened by other-card Negation tests.

**Completed identities retained for traceability:** `NEG-COPY-1 F1-R1/R2/R3` (historical original semantics/layout), `F1-R4/R5` (Duel physical duplicate/handoff), `R6A–D`, `R6-QA-01/02/03`, `R6-ARCH-01` and `R6-ARCH-02` form the delivered Duel-path and shared presentation baseline. #### 10.25.4 — NEXT OWNER TEST: Negation with other stratagem cards (NOT TESTED / REVIEW-ONLY)

The user intends to test **other stratagems' real Negation chains next**, including original Bumper Harvest recipient-specific interruption. This is a **new review boundary, not evidence of failure and not an authorization for Coding Agent implementation**. The user can test cards one at a time on the deployed Worker, using these acceptance questions:
- Does the genuine root card display once and park under its true author while other viewers see the same public event?
- At each committed first Negation / linked counter / proven final outcome, does H4 show the **actual** cancellation/restoration/blocking for that specific stratagem and recipient, while PAC separately describes only the next possible response?
- When the timer ends or a private Pass/Negation is locked, does the server-confirmed window — not client intent — control result, further response availability and authorized next action?
- Does TEST SWITCH preserve identical public meaning while keeping viewer-private Hand/choice information secret? Are genuine game effects, selected cards and private receipt semantics correct without duplicate physical cards or fake public announcements?
- Does the accepted R6 H4 fixed position, readable height, PAC-width/clock arrangement and usable Inspector/controls survive this different card's layout? For Harvest, pool pick remains read-only during Negation and only the proven current recipient's pick can be blocked/restored.

**Decision boundary:** Record `PASS`, `FAIL` or `NOT TESTED` **per observed card/interaction**; do not globally mark all other stratagems accepted from a single Duel run. A reported failing card is reviewed against the accepted shared R6 contract first and receives only an evidence-backed bounded correction under its original card/Negation scope if needed; accepted Duel and ARCH-02 remain closed. No new Coding Agent task or reimplementation is authorized until the owner supplies and approves the next specific test/fix. Existing focused regression, exact-SHA CI/deployment and optional ≤5-min **total local UI/browser testing per bounded task** remain mandatory for any future code change, not for this editorial acceptance update.
